Can we implement smooth mirror descent effectively? Let's formulate.

Let x_i = log a_i (with a_i = exp(x)/sum exp x). We want minimize objective M = max c_k. Use smooth F_β = logsumexp(β c)/β. But β too high causes spikes; too low slow. We can do a cyclic schedule of lags where c_k near max: for top L lags, adjust multiplicatively. For each lag m, update a_i *= exp(-η τ_m * (2 a_{m-i}))/normalize, with τ_m weighted by c_m^p or active. This is like coordinate descent on max? Algorithm "active lag multiplicative descent":
Given a (sum1) finite. Compute c=conv(a,a) via FFT. M=max(c). Choose lags with c_k ≥ M - δ M (e.g., 5% or top 20). For each active lag m with weight w_k = c_k^p (p maybe 2) / sum weights, perform multiplicative update on a:
   shift = a_reflected: r_j = a_{m-j} if 0≤m-j<n else 0.
   a *= exp(-η * w * r)
Normalize a/sum.
Maybe project [0,1000] automatically. This descent decreases c_m? Large r means high opposite and high self. It equalizes pairs. With multiple lags, could break uniform. Use η adaptive: try η in [0.01,0.1], evaluate surrogate M. Accept if M decreases; else reduce η. This is similar to gradient descent in mirror metric.

But is it guaranteed to uniform? For a uniform, r for central lag uniform; update a*=exp(-η uniform) leaves proportion uniform (no change!). For top lags including central and adjacent, r differs at edges, so nonuniform update occurs. For uniform, choose top lags central; weights by c (triangular), r intervals. The average effect: edges with fewer lags? This will increase edges? Let's estimate: For central m=n-1, r all same => no relative change. For m=n-2, r valid j=0..n-2 (a_{n-1}=0 at j=n-1? r_j=a_{n-2-j}; valid j=0..n-2; r_{n-1}=0), so update reduces j=0..n-2 and leaves last edge. Thus the last endpoint gets relative increase. Similarly m=n gives first endpoint increase. Weighted, endpoints increase, interior decreases. Good! This breaks uniform and may form U-shape. Then active lags shift, iterative could optimize.

This multiplicative active-lag method is O(L n) per iteration plus FFT. Could run for large n and many iterations. It may be quite effective for global score. Potential code architecture:
```
import numpy as np
import time, math
from scipy.signal import fftconvolve  # if available
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize? maybe.

def safe_conv(a):
   # a ndarray float64 1D normalized maybe
   # Use fftconvolve for n>512 else np.convolve
   if a.size < 512: return np.convolve(a,a)
   c = fftconvolve(a,a, mode='full')
   c[c<0 & small]=0 # numerical
   return c

def ac1_score(a, use_exact=False):
   S=a.sum()
   if S<0.01: return inf
   c=safe_conv(a)
   M=c.max()
   return float(2*a.size*M/(S*S))

def normalize(a, s=1.0):
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   S=a.sum()
   if S<=0: return ones
   return a*(s/S)
```

Exact evaluator `evaluate_sequence` uses np.convolve. We can call it for final, but also implement internal exact for best maybe with np.convolve to avoid overhead list conversion. `evaluate_sequence` expects list; we can make exact_eval_np(a) using same convolve for efficiency:
```
def exact_score_np(a):
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   if a.sum()<0.01: return inf
   c=np.convolve(a,a)
   return 2*a.size*c.max()/a.sum()**2
```
This matches evaluator (Clipping already). Use for validation every maybe 100 steps.

Let's design mirror active-lag algorithm in detail.

```
def mirror_descent(a, n, rng, time_limit, best_holder, temperature_rel sequence, eta start):
    a=normalize(a)
    best_a_local=a copy; best_score_local=exact maybe initially surrogate.
    eta=0.05
    temp_rel=0.02
    stagn=0
    for it in range(max):
       if time > limit: break
       c=fftconvolve(a,a) # nonnegative maybe
       M=c.max()
       if M <=0: break
       # λ: exp((c-M)/(M*temp_rel)) for c>0
       z = (c - M)/(M*temp_rel)  # <=0
       lam = np.exp(np.clip(z, -50, 0))
       lam_sum=lam.sum()
       if lam_sum==0: lam=np.zeros; lam[argmax]=1
       lam/=lam_sum
       b=2*correlate(a, lam) # length n, nonnegative
       # scale
       bmax=b.max()
       if bmax<=0: break
       a_new = a * np.exp(np.clip(-eta * b / bmax, -8, 8))
       a_new = normalize(a_new)
       # surrogate score
       c2=fftconvolve(a2,a2); M2=c2.max(); score2=2*n*M2
       if score2 < best_score_local - tol:
          accept; best_score_local=score2; exact periodically
          eta = min(eta*1.1, 0.6); temp_rel maybe adjust
       else:
          eta *= 0.7; temp_rel = max(temp_rel*0.7, 0.002) or increase? If no improvement, reduce. After many fails, reset with random perturbation.
          stagn += 1
          if stagn>20: reseed a = 0.8*a +0.2*random; eta=0.1, temp_rel=0.02; stagn=0
    return best
```

Need correlate function using FFT. Simpler: Use direct loop if active λ sparse? λ dense but decays; compute b via convolution with lamb using FFT.

Implement `correlate(a,w)`:
```
def correlate(a,w):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1
    # full conv of a and w[::-1]
    N=1<<(3*n-1).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,N)
    # b_i=c[L-1-i]
    return c[L-n:L][::-1]  # check indices
```
Let's verify with small indices. a length n, w length L=2n-1. conv_full index s from 0 to n+L-2=3n-2. b_i (i=0..n-1) desired at s=L-1-i. For i=n-1, s=L-n= (2n-1)-n=n-1. So indices from L-1 down to n-1 inclusive. Python slice `c[L-1 : n-2 : -1]`? Inclusive n-1. `c[L-1 : n-2 : -1]` stops before n-2, length n. Example n=3,L=5: desired s for i0=4,i1=3,i2=2. `c[4:1:-1]` gives [c4,c3,c2] (stops before 1). Good. Use `c[L-1 : n-2 : -1]` or `c[n-1:L][::-1]` (since indices n-1..L-1). `c[n-1:L][::-1]` clearer.
Test with n=3, a=[a0,a1,a2], w=[w0,w1,w2,w3,w4]. b0 = a0 w0 + a1 w1 + a2 w2. convull s=4: sum a_r w_rev[4-r], w_rev=[w4,w3,w2,w1,w0], r=0 w_rev[4]=w0; r=1 w_rev[3]=w1; r=2 w_rev[2]=w2. yes. b2 at s=2: r=0 w_rev2=w2; r=1 w_rev1=w3; r=2 w_rev0=w4 => a0 w2+a1 w3+a2 w4. desired b_i=sum_r a_r w_{i+r}: i=2 -> a0w2+a1w3+a2w4. Good.

```
def objective(x, lam_type='power', p=5, temp maybe):
```

Custom mirror descent on x with power objective:
`a=softmax(x)`. Gradient g = a*(b - avg). We can use adaptive RMS step (Adam):
```
m=beta1*m+(1-beta1)g; v=beta2*v+(1-beta2)g^2
x += lr * m / sqrt(v+eps)
x -= x.mean()
a = softmax(x)
```
This is fast and handles nonconvex. Use p schedule and temperature. We can evaluate score exactly/surrogate each iteration. For n=6733, one iter: convolve a (FFT rfft length 2^? 32768) ~0.01s; correlate another FFT maybe 0.01; total 0.02s per Adam update. 10000 iter 200s. Good. We can run many diverse lrs/p. This may outperform L-BFGS due speed. Use sub-sampling? Full objective.

However, objective gradient of power mean may have many local minima; Adam with restarts and random init could find low. We can incorporate random resets when stuck.

No filesystem, network.

Helpers:
- `clean_array(arr)`: convert, clip nonnegative, normalize.
- `evaluate_np(a)`: exact as evaluator (list not needed): clip, sum, np.convolve.
- `next_pow2(m)`.
- `fft_conv_cor(a, w, N)`: given N length, compute c and b.
```
def conv_corr(a, w, N):
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    fa_w=fa*fa
    c_full=np.fft.irfft(fa_w,N)
    c=c_full[:2*n-1]
    if w is not None:
       fw=np.fft.rfft(w[::-1],N)
       cf=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw,N)
       b=cf[n-1:2*n-1][::-1] # check slice for length n
    return c,b
```
For correlate, need N ≥ 3n-1. `cf[n-1:2*n-1]` length n? For n=3, indices 2,3,4 yes. If N large, okay. But `w` length L=2n-1. Good. For c_full, N≥2n-1; using N≥3n-1 fine.
- `surrogate_score(a,c)`: S=a.sum(); cmax=max(c,0); return 2*n*cmax/(S*S). If S<1e-12 inf.

Optimization `mirror_run(a, n, N, time_limit, rng, best_holder, init_power?)`:
Use local variables. Need avoid excessive allocations? okay.
```
def mirror_run(a, n, time_limit, best_holder, rng, verbose=False):
    N = next_pow2(3*n-1)
    a = a.astype(float).copy(); S=a.sum(); if S<=0 return a
    a = a/S; score=surrogate
    local_best=a.copy(); local_score=score
    exact_best = best_holder[1]
    eta = 0.1; power=3.0; top_frac=0.02
    no_improve=0; it=0; exact_it=0
    while time.monotonic() < time_limit:
       # choose power adapt
       if it < 500: power=2
       elif it <2000: power=4
       elif it <5000: power=6
       else: power=8
       # occasionally random
       if rng.random()<0.1: power=rng.choice([2,4,6,8,10])
       c,b? compute c
       c,_=conv_corr(a, None, N)
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
       score=surrogate_score(a,c)
       if score < local_score: update; if score < exact_best - 1e-8: exact? 
       if score > 1e8 break
       M = c.max()+1e-15
       cc = n*c  # maybe no need
       ratio = c/M
       # choose top vs power
       top_mode = rng.random() < 0.35
       if top_mode:
          L=c.size; K=int(max(8, L*rng.uniform(0.005,0.03)))
          idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
          w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=ratio[idx]**power
       else:
          w=ratio**power
       w+=1e-14; wsum=w.sum(); if wsum>0: w/=wsum
       c2,b=conv_corr(a,w,N) # reuse c? We already computed c; conv_corr will recompute fa and c. To avoid, create function that takes fa? But okay. We can compute fa once, c and b in one call.
```
Optimize: `conv_corr_with_fa`:
```
def get_c_b(a,w,N,n):
   fa=rfft(a,N)
   c=irfft(fa*fa,N)[:L]
   b=irfft(fa*rfft(w[::-1],N),N)[n-1:2n-1][::-1] if w is not None else None
   return c,b
```

```
LP implementation details:
```
def lp_direction(a, M):
   n=len(a); L=2*n-1
   A=np.empty((L,n), dtype=np.float64)
   for k in range(L):
      lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n-1,k)
      if lo<=hi:
         A[k, lo:hi+1] = a[k-hi:k-lo+1][::-1]
   # Note a must be current nonnegative; if some zero, matrix rows maybe zero; LP bounded? If a_i=0, variable g_j may only appear in rows with positive a? Bounded eventually. If all positive, ok.
   res=linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.full(L,M), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs')
   if res.status!=0: return None
   g=res.x
   S=g.sum(); if S<=0: return None
   return g/S
```
Then iterate:
```
def lp_optimize(a, n, time_limit, best_holder):
  a=a copy norm; score=exact/surrogate
  N=next_pow2(3n-1)
  t=0.2; stale=0
  while now<limit:
     c,_=get_c_b(a,None); M=c.max()
     if M<=0 break
     g=lp_direction(a,M)
     if g is None: break
     # line search
     improved=False
     for tt in (t,0.5*t,0.25*t,0.1*t):
        a_new=(1-tt)*a+tt*g; normalize
        c_new; score_new
        if score_new < score: accept; improved=True; t=min(t*1.1,0.5); break
     if improved: update exact; stale=0
     else:
        t*=0.7; stale+=1
        if stale>10: break
```

Let's detail helper exact_score:
```
def exact_score_np(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    S = float(a.sum())
    if S < 0.01 or a.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(a).all(): return math.inf
    c = np.convolve(a,a)
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0
    if not math.isfinite(M): return math.inf
    return float(2*a.size*M/(S*S))
```
This matches evaluator after clipping. If values >1000 clipped; our values <=1 so fine.

FFT helper:
```
def next_pow2(x): return 1 << (int(x)-1).bit_length() if x>1 else 1

def get_conv_corr(a, w, N, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    cfull = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
    L=2*n-1
    c = cfull[:L]
    # numerical round small negative
    # b
    if w is None: return c, None
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
    y = np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, N)
    # slice
    b = y[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
    return c, b
```
Check slice length N: Need N >= 2n-1; n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive length n. If N maybe exactly? pow2 >=3n-1 >2n, okay. Floating errors may produce imaginary? irfft real.
We should set `c = np.maximum(c,0)`? For surrogate max, small negative near zero not issue. But if all negative? c[0]=a0^2 positive. Use `c = np.where(c>0,c,0)`? This costs; do maybe `c[c<0]=0` modifies array. Use `if (c<0).any(): c=np.maximum(c,0)`. For max, negative ignored automatically. Use `M=np.max(c)`; if some negative no matter. For weights ratio, negative issue; after M, `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`. So in mirror after get c: `c = np.maximum(c,0.0)`.

`surrogate_score(a,c,n)`: S=a.sum(); if S<1e-12 inf; M=max(c); return 2*n*M/S*S.
```

def consider_candidate(a):
   if a is None: return
   a2 = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
   if a2.size==0 or not finite: return
   a2=np.clip(a2,0,1000)
   S=a2.sum()
   if S<0.01: return
   a2 = a2/S  # scale invariant? Evaluator scale invariant except clipping; normalizing doesn't change score but ensures no cap. If some values >1000? scaling down okay.
   sc=exact_score_np(a2) # it clips/scopes, but we already normalized
   if sc < best['score'] - 1e-12:
       best['score']=sc; best['a']=a2.copy()
```

Let's implement mirror with a fixed `N` and local `best_local` (surrogate) and `last_exact`.
```
def mirror_run(a_in, n, time_limit, rng, consider):
    a = normalize(a_in,n)
    N = next_pow2(3*n - 1)
    c,_ = get_conv_corr(a,None,N,n); c=[max0]
    score=surrogate
    local_best=a.copy(); local_score=score
    eta=0.15; temp_rel=0.05

```
    x = x0.astype(float).copy()

    heat=np.zeros(n); coldheat=np.zeros(n); hot_idx=np.arange(0); cold_idx=np.arange(0); K=max(8,n//200)
    def recompute_indices(): ...
```

For modular mask:
```
def modular_mask(n, rng):
    i=np.arange(n,dtype=np.int64)
    a = int(rng.integers(1, max(2,n)))
    b = int(rng.integers(0,n))
    r=(a*i+b)%n
    q=(r*r)%n
    k=n//2
    x=q<k
    # If 0? maybe include if rng<0.5? no
    return x.astype(float)
```
If n even, q< n/2 density about0.5 (if prime). Good.

`fft_c` function:
```
def fft_c(a, N, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    cfull = np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, N)
    return cfull[:2*n-1], fa
```
This computes circular convolution of length N; because N >= 2n-1, first 2n-1 are linear. Good.

`surrogate_from_c(a,c,n)`:
```
S=a.sum(); M=c.max(); if S<=0 or not finite: inf; return 2*n*M/(S*S)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(x)
    if x <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (x - 1).bit_length()


def _normalize(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size != n:
        if a.size > n:
            a = a[:n]
        else:
            b = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            b[:a.size] = a
            a = b
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _exact_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return math.inf
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return math.inf
    c = np.convolve(a, a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _fft_c(a, N, n):
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    cf = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    return cf[:2 * n - 1], fa


def _surrogate_from_c(a, c, n):
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return math.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not math.isfinite(m):
        return math.inf
    return 2.0 * n * m / (s * s)


def _pool_score(a, n, N):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size != n:
        a = _normalize(a, n)
    c, _ = _fft_c(a, N, n)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return _surrogate_from_c(a, c, n)


def _soft_from_binary(x, strength=8.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    logits = strength * (x - 0.5)
    logits -= np.max(logits)
    a = np.exp(logits)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(len(x), 1.0 / len(x), dtype=np.float64)
    return a / s


def _binary_mask_random(n, p=0.5, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    return (rng.random(n) < p).astype(np.float64)


def _binary_mask_modular(n, rng):
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    a = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, n)))
    b = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n)))
    r = (a * i + b) % n
    q = (r * r) % n
    x = (q < n // 2).astype(np.float64)
    if int(x.sum()) < n // 10:
        x = (q < n // 2 + 1).astype(np.float64)
    return x


def _binary_swap_opt(x0, n, N, deadline, rng, consider):
    x = np.asarray(x0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    k = int(round(float(x.sum())))
    if k < max(8, n // 20) or k > n - 8:
        return None

    c, _ = _fft_c(x, N, n)
    c = np.rint(np.maximum(c, 0.0))
    score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max()) / float(k * k)
    if not math.isfinite(score):
        return None

    best_x = x.copy()
    best_score = score

    L = 2 * n - 1
    K = max(4, min(L // 2, n // 160))
    heat = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    coldheat = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    def refresh_weights():
        if c.size > 2 * K:
            hot = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
            cold = np.argpartition(c, K)[:K]
        else:
            hot = np.arange(c.size)
            cold = np.arange(c.size)
        heat[:] = 0.0
        coldheat[:] = 0.0
        for m in hot:
            lo = max(0, m - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, m)
            if lo <= hi:
                heat[lo:hi + 1] += x[m - hi:m - lo + 1][::-1]
        for m in cold:
            lo = max(0, m - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, m)
            if lo <= hi:
                coldheat[lo:hi + 1] += x[m - hi:m - lo + 1][::-1]

    refresh_weights()
    it = 0
    since_refresh = 0
    refresh_every = max(60, n // 80)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if it == 0 or since_refresh >= refresh_every:
            refresh_weights()
            since_refresh = 0

        ones = np.flatnonzero(x > 0.5)
        if ones.size == 0:
            break
        zeros = np.flatnonzero(x <= 0.5)
        if zeros.size == 0:
            break

        h1 = heat[ones]
        h1s = float(h1.sum())
        if h1s > 1e-12 and rng.random() < 0.92:
            p1 = h1 / h1s
            j = int(rng.choice(ones, p=p1))
        else:
            j = int(rng.choice(ones))

        benefit = coldheat[zeros] - 0.35 * heat[zeros]
        benefit = np.maximum(benefit, 0.0)
        bs = float(benefit.sum())
        if bs > 1e-12 and rng.random() < 0.92:
            pz = benefit / bs
            l = int(rng.choice(zeros, p=pz))
        else:
            l = int(rng.choice(zeros))

        if j == l:
            it += 1
            since_refresh += 1
            continue

        mag = 2.0 * x
        mag[j] = 1.0
        c[j:j + n] -= mag
        x[j] = 0.0

        mag2 = 2.0 * x
        mag2[l] = 1.0
        c[l:l + n] += mag2

        mx = float(c.max())
        ns = 2.0 * n * mx / float(k * k)

        if math.isfinite(ns) and ns < best_score - 1e-12:
            x[l] = 1.0
            best_score = ns
            best_x = x.copy()
            if it % 2000 == 0 and k > 0:
                consider(best_x / float(k))
        else:
            c[l:l + n] -= mag2
            c[j:j + n] += mag
            x[j] = 1.0

        it += 1
        since_refresh += 1
        if it % 2000 == 0:
            c = np.rint(np.maximum(c, 0.0))
            score = 2.0 * n * float(c.max()) / float(k * k)
            if not math.isfinite(score) or score > 1e8:
                break

    if best_x is not None and k > 0:
        consider(best_x / float(k))
    return _soft_from_binary(best_x, strength=9.0)


def _mirror_run(a0, n, N, deadline, rng, consider):
    a = _normalize(a0, n)
    local_best = a.copy()
    local_score = math.inf

    c, fa = _fft_c(a, N, n)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    score = _surrogate_from_c(a, c, n)
    if math.isfinite(score):
        local_score = score
        local_best = a.copy()

    eta = 0.25
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_exact = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if not math.isfinite(score) or score > 1e10:
            a = local_best.copy()
            c, fa = _fft_c(a, N, n)
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            score = _surrogate_from_c(a, c, n)
            local_score = score
            no_improve = 0

        m = float(np.max(c))
        if not math.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
            break

        max_cc = m * n
        if max_cc > 1.50:
            temp_rel = float(rng.uniform(0.004, 0.020))
            use_top = rng.random() < 0.65
        elif max_cc > 1.20:
            temp_rel = float(rng.uniform(0.010, 0.050))
            use_top = rng.random() < 0.50
        else:
            temp_rel = float(rng.uniform(0.050, 0.160))
            use_top = rng.random() < 0.35

        denom = m * temp_rel
        z = (c - m) / (denom + 1e-300)
        w = np.exp(np.clip(z, -80.0, 0.0))

        if use_top:
            L = c.size
            K = int(max(6, L * float(rng.uniform(0.003, 0.020))))
            if K >= L:
                idx = np.arange(L)
            else:
                idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
            mask = np.zeros(L, dtype=bool)
            mask[idx] = True
            w *= mask

        ws = float(w.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros_like(c)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= ws

        fw = np.fft.rfft(w[::-1], N)
        b = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, N)
        b = 2.0 * b[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]

        if not np.isfinite(b).all():
            break

        bmean = float(np.mean(b))
        bscale = float(np.max(b - bmean))
        if not math.isfinite(bscale) or bscale <= 1e-18:
            no_improve += 20
            if no_improve > 50:
                sig = 0.05 + 0.15 * float(rng.random())
                a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
                a = _normalize(a, n)
                c, fa = _fft_c(a, N, n)
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
                score = _surrogate_from_c(a, c, n)
                no_improve = 0
            continue

        e = -eta * (b - bmean) / bscale
        e = np.clip(e, -5.0, 5.0)
        a_new = a * np.exp(e)
        s = float(a_new.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.5)
            no_improve += 10
            continue
        a_new /= s

        c_new, _ = _fft_c(a_new, N, n)
        c_new = np.maximum(c_new, 0.0)
        score_new = _surrogate_from_c(a_new, c_new, n)

        if math.isfinite(score_new) and score_new < score - 1e-12:
            a = a_new
            c = c_new
            score = score_new
            no_improve = 0
            eta = min(0.8, eta * 1.04)
            if score < local_score - 1e-15:
                local_score = score
                local_best = a.copy()
        else:
            eta = max(0.02, eta * 0.78)
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 180:
                sig = 0.04 + 0.22 * float(rng.random())
                a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
                a = _normalize(a, n)
                c, fa = _fft_c(a, N, n)
                c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
                score = _surrogate_from_c(a, c, n)
                if math.isfinite(score) and score < local_score - 1e-15:
                    local_score = score
                    local_best = a.copy()
                eta = min(0.6, max(eta, 0.2))
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1
        if (it % 450 == 0) or (score < local_score - 1e-12 and it % 30 == 0):
            if time.monotonic() - last_exact > 5.0:
                consider(a)
                last_exact = time.monotonic()

        if a.max() > 0.999:
            a = local_best.copy()
            c, fa = _fft_c(a, N, n)
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            score = _surrogate_from_c(a, c, n)
            local_score = score
            no_improve += 30

    consider(a)
    return a, local_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        raw_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        raw_budget = 1300.0
    raw_budget = max(10.0, raw_budget)
    deadline = t0 + max(5.0, raw_budget - 20.0)

    best = {"a": None, "score": math.inf}

    def consider(a):
        try:
            a = _normalize(np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64), int(np.asarray(a).size) if a is not None else 1)
            sc = _exact_score(a)
            if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best["score"] - 1e-12:
                best["score"] = sc
                best["a"] = a.copy()
        except Exception:
            pass

    try:
        parent = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None and parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size > 0:
        parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if parent.sum() > 0:
            parent = _normalize(parent, parent.size)
            consider(parent)
        else:
            parent = None
    else:
        parent = None

    if parent is None:
        n = 4096
    else:
        n = int(parent.size)
        if n > 10000:
            n = 8192
        if n < 256:
            n = 256

    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 1)
    base = parent if parent is not None else np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    base = _normalize(base, n)

    pool = []

    def add_pool(a):
        try:
            a = _normalize(a, n)
            if not np.isfinite(a).all():
                return
            consider(a)
            sc = _pool_score(a, n, N)
            if math.isfinite(sc):
                pool.append((a, sc))
        except Exception:
            pass

    add_pool(base)

    if parent is not None:
        pert = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n))
        add_pool(pert)

    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)

    add_pool((rng.exponential(1.0, n) + 0.05))

    for _ in range(3):
        ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.2831853))
        ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 6.2831853))
        a1 = 1.0 + 0.75 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * i / n + ph1)
        a2 = a1 * (1.0 + 0.35 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * i / n + ph2))
        a2 = np.maximum(a2, 1e-4)
        add_pool(a2)

    for alpha in (0.5, 0.9, 1.2):
        ramp = 1.0 + alpha * (i / max(1.0, n - 1.0) - 0.5)
        add_pool(np.maximum(ramp, 1e-4))

    if raw_budget > 420 and n <= 8192:
        now = time.monotonic()
        bin_budget = min(135.0, 0.11 * raw_budget)
        bin_budget = min(bin_budget, max(0.0, deadline - now - 170.0))
        if bin_budget > 30.0:
            seg = bin_budget * 0.5
            m1 = _binary_mask_modular(n, rng)
            soft = _binary_swap_opt(m1, n, N, now + seg, rng, consider)
            if soft is not None:
                add_pool(soft)

            if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
                m2 = _binary_mask_random(n, p=0.5, rng=rng)
                soft2 = _binary_swap_opt(m2, n, N, time.monotonic() + seg, rng, consider)
                if soft2 is not None:
                    add_pool(soft2)

    pool.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
    initial_pool = pool[:6]

    for a, _sc in initial_pool:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 45.0:
            break
        left = max(0.0, deadline - now - 45.0)
        phase = min(55.0, max(12.0, left / max(1.0, len(initial_pool))))
        if phase <= 0.0:
            break
        res, rsc = _mirror_run(a, n, N, now + phase, rng, consider)
        if res is not None and math.isfinite(rsc):
            pool.append((res, rsc))

    pool.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
    if pool:
        long_a = pool[0][0]
    else:
        long_a = base

    res, rsc = _mirror_run(long_a, n, N, deadline, rng, consider)
    if res is not None and math.isfinite(rsc):
        pool.append((res, rsc))
        pool.sort(key=lambda x: x[1])
        if pool[0][1] < (rsc + 1e-12):
            consider(pool[0][0])

    if best["a"] is None or not math.isfinite(best["score"]):
        best["a"] = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
        best["score"] = _exact_score(best["a"])

    out = np.asarray(best["a"], dtype=np.float64)
    out = _normalize(out, out.size)
    if out.size == 0 or not np.isfinite(out).all():
        out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0

    try:
        fscore = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out]))
        if not math.isfinite(fscore):
            out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
            fscore = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out]))
            if not math.isfinite(fscore):
                out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0
    except Exception:
        sc = _exact_score(out)
        if not math.isfinite(sc):
            out = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
